<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">01</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://openai.com/index/introducing-gpt-6-1-sol/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>GPT 6.1 Sol</span><span class='zh'>GPT-6.1 Sol 发布</span></a><span class="dom">openai.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenAI introduces GPT-6.1 Sol as an incremental update to the GPT-6 line, focusing on improved reliability, safety controls, and developer tools.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 发布了 GPT-6.1 Sol，作为 GPT-6 系列的增量更新，着力提升稳定性与安全控制，并强化对开发者工具的支持。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenAI introduces GPT-6.1 Sol as an incremental update to the GPT-6 line, focusing on improved reliability, safety controls, and developer tools. The release signals a continued push toward more capable, safer AI APIs with tighter integration options for application builders.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 发布了 GPT-6.1 Sol，作为 GPT-6 系列的增量更新，着力提升稳定性与安全控制，并强化对开发者工具的支持。这一发布继续推动更强的能力与更高的安全性，并提供更紧密的应用集成能力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/09/draftkings-using-ai-supercharge-harms-online-behavioral-advertising" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>DraftKings Is Using AI to Behaviorally Target Chronic Gamblers</span><span class='zh'>DraftKings 正在用 AI 针对慢性赌徒进行行为定向</span></a><span class="dom">eff.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>DraftKings is using AI to tailor advertising based on chronic gamblers’ behavior, aiming to maximize engagement and spending.</span><span class='zh'>DraftKings 正在利用 AI 根据慢性赌徒的行为特征对广告进行定向，以提升参与度和支出。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>DraftKings is using AI to tailor advertising based on chronic gamblers’ behavior, aiming to maximize engagement and spending. This raises urgent ethical and regulatory concerns about predatory targeting, calling for stronger safeguards, transparency, and oversight to protect vulnerable users.</span><span class='zh'>DraftKings 正在利用 AI 根据慢性赌徒的行为特征对广告进行定向，以提升参与度和支出。这一做法引发关于掠夺性定向、伦理与监管的紧迫关切，需加强保护措施、透明度和监管以保护脆弱用户。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>macOS Golden Gate is described as a buggy, unstable feature with multiple faults impacting user experience.</span><span class='zh'>文中指出 macOS 的 Golden Gate 存在大量漏洞与不稳定性，影响用户体验。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>macOS Golden Gate is described as a buggy, unstable feature with multiple faults impacting user experience.</span><span class='zh'>文中指出 macOS 的 Golden Gate 存在大量漏洞与不稳定性，影响用户体验。报道强调测试不足和频繁更新带来的可靠性风险，呼吁改进测试、提供回滚选项并公布更清晰的版本说明。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">05</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.derekthompson.org/p/the-death-of-the-american-host" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>You are no longer invited to dinner</span><span class='zh'>再无人请你吃饭</span></a><span class="dom">derekthompson.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>America’s traditional hosting culture is fading as social rituals shrink and people gravitate toward streaming, delivery, or private get-togethers.</span><span class='zh'>美国传统的待客文化正在式微，社交仪式缩减，人们转向流媒体、外卖或私密聚会。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>America’s traditional hosting culture is fading as social rituals shrink and people gravitate toward streaming, delivery, or private get-togethers. The piece argues this shift reshapes community ties and opens room for new kinds of social tools and services, from event platforms to hospitality apps.</span><span class='zh'>美国传统的待客文化正在式微，社交仪式缩减，人们转向流媒体、外卖或私密聚会。该文指出这一转变重塑社区联系，同时为新型社交工具与服务—如活动平台和待客行业应用—留出空间。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">06</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://spectrum.ieee.org/delhi-electricity-loss" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>How Delhi cut electricity loss from 50 to 5 percent</span><span class='zh'>德里如何将电力损耗从50%降至5%</span></a><span class="dom">spectrum.ieee.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Delhi dramatically reduced electricity distribution losses from about 50% to around 5% through targeted reforms, grid upgrades, and better metering.</span><span class='zh'>德里通过有针对性的改革、输配电网升级和改进计量，将电力损耗从约50%降至约5%左右。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Delhi dramatically reduced electricity distribution losses from about 50% to around 5% through targeted reforms, grid upgrades, and better metering. The move showcases how smart infrastructure and governance can unlock huge efficiency gains for urban utilities and could serve as a scalable model for other cities.</span><span class='zh'>德里通过有针对性的改革、输配电网升级和改进计量，将电力损耗从约50%降至约5%左右。此举展示了智慧基础设施与治理在提升城市电力效率方面的潜力，并具有可复制性供其他城市参考。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theguardian.com/technology/2026/sep/29/trial-live-facial-recognition-cameras-london-stations-false-positive" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>500k facial scans at UK stations yield no arrests, 1 false positive</span><span class='zh'>英国车站的五十万次人脸识别未逮捕，仅一例误报</span></a><span class="dom">theguardian.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A large-scale facial recognition test at UK rail stations produced no arrests and just one false positive from half a million scans.</span><span class='zh'>英国铁路站点的大规模人脸识别测试在累计约50万次扫描中未逮捕任何人，仅有一次误报。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A large-scale facial recognition test at UK rail stations produced no arrests and just one false positive from half a million scans. The result highlights limited predictive value for crime prevention and rekindles debates over privacy and surveillance.</span><span class='zh'>英国铁路站点的大规模人脸识别测试在累计约50万次扫描中未逮捕任何人，仅有一次误报。结果凸显对治安预防的实际效用有限，再次引发对隐私与监控的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.tomshardware.com/software/the-netherlands-is-rolling-alternative-nixos-based-software-ecosystem-after-u-s-sanctions-on-icc-took-microsoft-off-the-table-trial-programs-running-now-first-release-expected-at-end-of-2027" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>US sanctions force The Netherlands off Microsoft and toward alternative NixOS</span><span class='zh'>美国制裁迫使荷兰放弃微软，转向 NixOS 为基础的替代方案</span></a><span class="dom">tomshardware.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>US sanctions are nudging the Netherlands to move away from Microsoft toward an open-source NixOS-based software ecosystem.</span><span class='zh'>美国制裁促使荷兰摆脱微软，转向以 NixOS 为基础的开源软件生态。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>US sanctions are nudging the Netherlands to move away from Microsoft toward an open-source NixOS-based software ecosystem. The shift could affect government procurement, software sovereignty, and ongoing modernization efforts.</span><span class='zh'>美国制裁促使荷兰摆脱微软，转向以 NixOS 为基础的开源软件生态。此举可能影响政府采购、软件主权以及持续的现代化进程。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://status.claude.com/incidents/4xvtc2gnq73l" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude partial outage</span><span class='zh'>Claude 部分性中断</span></a><span class="dom">status.claude.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Claude experienced a partial outage that affected a subset of users or features.</span><span class='zh'>Claude 遭遇部分性中断，影响到部分用户或功能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Claude experienced a partial outage that affected a subset of users or features. The incident underscores how even major AI services can face reliability challenges, highlighting the importance of robust incident response and communication for enterprise users.</span><span class='zh'>Claude 遭遇部分性中断，影响到部分用户或功能。事件凸显即使是大型人工智能服务也会遇到可靠性挑战，强调对企业用户在故障响应与沟通方面的必要性。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://jorgegarciaherrero.com/wp-content/interactivos/20260916-Prompt-like-a-butterfly-sting-like-a-tracker-(clean).pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=jorgegarciaherrero.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>jorgegarciaherrero.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">10</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://jorgegarciaherrero.com/wp-content/interactivos/20260916-Prompt-like-a-butterfly-sting-like-a-tracker-(clean).pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A Privacy Analysis of Web and Mobile Conversational AI Agents [pdf]</span><span class='zh'>网络与移动端对话式人工智能代理的隐私分析</span></a><span class="dom">jorgegarciaherrero.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A detailed privacy analysis of web and mobile conversational AI agents reveals how these systems collect data, process transcripts, and may expose users to privacy risks.</span><span class='zh'>对网页及移动端对话式人工智能代理的数据收集、传输与存储等隐私风险进行了深入分析，揭示潜在的敏感信息泄露与第三方数据共享等问题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A detailed privacy analysis of web and mobile conversational AI agents reveals how these systems collect data, process transcripts, and may expose users to privacy risks. The report highlights issues like data sharing with third parties, retention policies, and potential prompt leakage, underscoring the need for stricter minimization, transparency, and privacy-preserving design.</span><span class='zh'>对网页及移动端对话式人工智能代理的数据收集、传输与存储等隐私风险进行了深入分析，揭示潜在的敏感信息泄露与第三方数据共享等问题。强调需要更严格的数据最小化、透明度与隐私保护设计，以及改进的访问控制与用户权利。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/PostHog/jeeves" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Jeeves. Reasoning improves Jev-like decision models</span><span class='zh'>Jeeves：推理能力提升 Jev 风格决策模型</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Jeeves introduces explicit reasoning to Jev-like decision models, boosting reliability and interpretability.</span><span class='zh'>Jeeves 将显式推理引入 Jev 风格的决策模型，提升可靠性与可解释性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Jeeves introduces explicit reasoning to Jev-like decision models, boosting reliability and interpretability. The project experiments with integrating step-by-step logic into AI-assisted analytics to improve decision quality.</span><span class='zh'>Jeeves 将显式推理引入 Jev 风格的决策模型，提升可靠性与可解释性。该项目尝试将逐步推理逻辑嵌入 AI 辅助分析中，以提高决策质量。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">14</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/firelex/jeff" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Jeff – Jev-compatible 0.8B decision models, trained at home, ~30 ms</span><span class='zh'>Jeff：Jev 兼容的0.8B决策模型，在家训练，推理约30毫秒</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Jeff introduces Jev-compatible 0.8B decision models that can be trained at home and run inference in about 30 ms.</span><span class='zh'>Jeff 提供 Jev 兼容的 0.8B 决策模型，可在本地训练，推理时延约 30 毫秒。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Jeff introduces Jev-compatible 0.8B decision models that can be trained at home and run inference in about 30 ms. The lightweight scale enables on-device experimentation, lowering barriers for individuals and small teams to prototype decision systems.</span><span class='zh'>Jeff 提供 Jev 兼容的 0.8B 决策模型，可在本地训练，推理时延约 30 毫秒。此举降低了个人和小团队在本地进行 AI 原型开发的门槛，凸显对隐私和本地化计算友好的趋势。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A call to audit AI labs’ practices, funding, and safety standards, advocating transparency and independent oversight of AI research and development.</span><span class='zh'>呼吁对 AI 实验室的实践、资金来源与安全标准进行审计，倡导透明度与独立监督以确保研究安全与合规。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://sancho.bearblog.dev/google-weird/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>When did Google get so weird?</span><span class='zh'>谷歌什么时候变得这么怪了？</span></a><span class="dom">sancho.bearblog.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google has shifted toward bolder experiments and AI-driven features across its products.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌在产品上变得更加大胆，推出基于 AI 的新功能与实验性改动。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google has shifted toward bolder experiments and AI-driven features across its products. The change reflects a broader industry push toward rapid iteration, sometimes at the expense of polish and user expectations.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌在产品上变得更加大胆，推出基于 AI 的新功能与实验性改动。这一趋势与行业对快速迭代的普遍追求相呼应，偶尔会牺牲产品的稳定性与用户期望。</span></p>

When compensation or guarantees hinge on volatile asset prices, personal finances become exposed to market swings and regulatory quirks.</span><span class='zh'>有人被要求以 Nvidia 股票作债务，金额高达十亿美元，揭示以股票为财富的现实风险。股价波动使基于股权的承诺带来流动性与合规方面的挑战，个人资产因此暴露于市场波动。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.alexewerlof.com/p/coding-is-not-solved" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Coding is not solved</span><span class='zh'>编码问题尚未解决</span></a><span class="dom">blog.alexewerlof.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Code remains a hard problem despite tooling progress; writing correct, maintainable software, with scalable architecture and solid processes, is still challenging.</span><span class='zh'>尽管工具与AI辅助不断进步，编码仍然是一个难题；正确、可维护且具可扩展性的系统设计与流程依然具有挑战性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Code remains a hard problem despite tooling progress; writing correct, maintainable software, with scalable architecture and solid processes, is still challenging. AI can help but cannot replace the craft of software engineering or the need for disciplined practices.</span><span class='zh'>尽管工具与AI辅助不断进步，编码仍然是一个难题；正确、可维护且具可扩展性的系统设计与流程依然具有挑战性。AI 能帮助，但无法取代软件工程的工艺性与规范性实践。</span></p>

Some farmers pivot to alternative uses like table grapes, juice, or raisins, illustrating how shifts in consumer tastes ripple through agriculture and supply chains.</span><span class='zh'>酒类需求下降导致加州葡萄种植者收入受压，葡萄价格走低、供应过剩。部分农民转向鲜食葡萄、果汁或葡萄干等替代用途，展现出消费者口味变化对农业生产和供应链的影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://oilprice.com/Latest-Energy-News/World-News/US-Strategic-Petroleum-Reserve-Falls-to-Lowest-Level-Since-1982.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>U.S. Strategic Petroleum Reserve Falls to Lowest Level Since 1982</span><span class='zh'>美国战略石油储备降至1982年以来的最低水平</span></a><span class="dom">oilprice.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The U.S. Strategic Petroleum Reserve has fallen to its lowest level since 1982 due to ongoing releases intended to stabilize fuel prices.</span><span class='zh'>美国战略石油储备因持续释放以稳定燃油价格，降至1982年以来的最低水平。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The U.S. Strategic Petroleum Reserve has fallen to its lowest level since 1982 due to ongoing releases intended to stabilize fuel prices. The depletion underscores the trade-off between short-term price stability and long-term energy security, with potential implications for future policy and market resilience.</span><span class='zh'>美国战略石油储备因持续释放以稳定燃油价格，降至1982年以来的最低水平。这折射出短期价格稳定与长期能源安全之间的权衡，并可能影响未来的政策走向与市场韧性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.ssp.sh/brain/the-problem-is-not-the-ai-code-but-nobody-knows-anything-anymore/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The problem is not AI code, but not knowing about system architecture or intent</span><span class='zh'>问题不在AI代码，而在于对体系结构与意图的缺乏了解</span></a><span class="dom">ssp.sh</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The real bottleneck isn’t the AI-generated code but humans’ lack of systemic context.</span><span class='zh'>真正的问题不是 AI 生成的代码，而是对系统架构、目标与上下文的缺乏清晰理解。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The real bottleneck isn’t the AI-generated code but humans’ lack of systemic context. Without solid architecture understanding and a clear intent, AI-assisted code can misfit, leading to brittle systems and governance gaps.</span><span class='zh'>真正的问题不是 AI 生成的代码，而是对系统架构、目标与上下文的缺乏清晰理解。缺乏架构层面的把握与明确意图，AI 产物容易不贴合需求，造成系统脆弱与治理漏洞。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece provocatively critiques Google&#x27;s handling of nude imagery and related privacy/content policies, arguing that platform decisions around sensitive media reflect broader tensions between user privacy and platform-scale data processing.</span><span class='zh'>文章以挑衅口吻讨论谷歌在处理裸体图像及相关隐私与内容政策方面的取舍，指出平台在敏感媒体处理上的决策折射出用户隐私与平台规模化数据处理之间的博弈。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece provocatively critiques Google&#x27;s handling of nude imagery and related privacy/content policies, arguing that platform decisions around sensitive media reflect broader tensions between user privacy and platform-scale data processing. It frames the shift as part of a larger trend toward more privacy-conscious content tooling.</span><span class='zh'>文章以挑衅口吻讨论谷歌在处理裸体图像及相关隐私与内容政策方面的取舍，指出平台在敏感媒体处理上的决策折射出用户隐私与平台规模化数据处理之间的博弈。并将这一转变置于日益注重隐私保护的内容工具趋势之中。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://thecivilian.co.nz/2026/09/27/ai-companies-in-fierce-arms-race-to-demonstrate-their-model-is-the-most-existentially-threatening-to-humanity/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI companies in race to demonstrate their model most threatening to humanity</span><span class='zh'>AI 公司竞相证明其模型对人类威胁最大</span></a><span class="dom">thecivilian.co.nz</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI firms are racing to demonstrate which model poses the greatest existential threat to humanity, a framing that influences policy, funding, and public trust.</span><span class='zh'>AI 公司正在竞相证明其模型对人类构成最大的生存威胁，这种论调会影响政策、资金与公众信任。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AI firms are racing to demonstrate which model poses the greatest existential threat to humanity, a framing that influences policy, funding, and public trust. This competition blends safety rhetoric with competitive signaling, sometimes obscuring real risk assessments. The takeaway is that governance and independent evaluation matter as much as capability.</span><span class='zh'>AI 公司正在竞相证明其模型对人类构成最大的生存威胁，这种论调会影响政策、资金与公众信任。此类竞赛把安全口号和竞争信号混合在一起，常常掩盖真实风险评估。关键在于治理与独立评估的重要性，与能力两者同等重要。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Data suggests Reddit hosts coordinated manipulation campaigns across topics; patterns include timing, cross-subreddit activity, and bot-like behavior.</span><span class='zh'>数据指出 Reddit 上存在协同行动的操纵化宣传现象，表现为跨子版块的时间安排、协同互动和机器人化行为等模式。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Data suggests Reddit hosts coordinated manipulation campaigns across topics; patterns include timing, cross-subreddit activity, and bot-like behavior. The findings highlight challenges in detecting astroturfing and reputational risk for communities.</span><span class='zh'>数据指出 Reddit 上存在协同行动的操纵化宣传现象，表现为跨子版块的时间安排、协同互动和机器人化行为等模式。这揭示了识别刷量造势的难点及对社区声誉的潜在风险。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://git.mills.io/prologic/parley" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Parley: Federated, decentralised chat that speaks plain IRC</span><span class='zh'>Parley：联邦化、去中心化的聊天，像 IRC 一样简单</span></a><span class="dom">git.mills.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Parley federates chat across servers using an IRC-like protocol, offering a decentralized alternative to centralized chat apps.</span><span class='zh'>Parley 将聊天跨服务器联邦化，使用类 IRC 的协议，提供一个去中心化的替代方案。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Parley federates chat across servers using an IRC-like protocol, offering a decentralized alternative to centralized chat apps. It emphasizes user ownership and interoperability over platform-lock-in. Adoption will depend on ecosystem tooling and client support.</span><span class='zh'>Parley 将聊天跨服务器联邦化，使用类 IRC 的协议，提供一个去中心化的替代方案。它强调用户掌控和互操作性，而非被单一平台锁定。能否被广泛采用取决于生态工具和客户端支持的成熟度。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Starship&#x27;s first orbital flight marks a landmark for fully reusable space launch tech, testing end-to-end performance from fueling to recovery.</span><span class='zh'>SpaceX 的 Starship 首次进入轨道发射标志着可完全重复使用的太空发射技术的里程碑，涵盖从加注到回收的端到端测试。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://arxiv.org/abs/2110.01834" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Thinking fast and slow in AI: The role of metacognition (2021)</span><span class='zh'>AI中的快思考与慢思考：元认知的作用（2021）</span></a><span class="dom">arxiv.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI systems can benefit from metacognitive capabilities—reflecting on their own reasoning, critiquing steps, and calibrating confidence.</span><span class='zh'>AI 系统若具备元认知能力，能够反思自身推理、纠错并校准信心。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AI systems can benefit from metacognitive capabilities—reflecting on their own reasoning, critiquing steps, and calibrating confidence. This line of research borrows cognitive science to improve reliability, safety, and interpretability in AI, helping models avoid overconfident errors.</span><span class='zh'>AI 系统若具备元认知能力，能够反思自身推理、纠错并校准信心。这类研究将认知科学引入机器学习，提升 AI 的可靠性、可解释性和安全性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.quora.com/Did-the-ENIAC-have-a-BIOS/answer/Alan-Kay-11" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Alan Kay&#x27;s answer to “Did the ENIAC have a BIOS”?</span><span class='zh'>艾伦·凯对“ENIAC是否有 BIOS”的回答</span></a><span class="dom">quora.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Alan Kay clarifies that ENIAC did not have a BIOS by modern definitions; it used hardware wiring and manual bootstrapping rather than firmware.</span><span class='zh'>艾伦·凯澄清，ENIAC 不具备现代 BIOS 的概念，它依靠硬件布线和手动引导，而非固件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Alan Kay clarifies that ENIAC did not have a BIOS by modern definitions; it used hardware wiring and manual bootstrapping rather than firmware. The note sheds light on how early machines booted and how software-era concepts evolved.</span><span class='zh'>艾伦·凯澄清，ENIAC 不具备现代 BIOS 的概念，它依靠硬件布线和手动引导，而非固件。此说法帮助理解早期计算机的启动机制，以及固件如何演化成我们熟悉的接口。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nytimes.com/2026/09/28/technology/openai-astra-safety.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenAI Says It Will Not Release Newest A.I. Model Over Safety Concerns</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 将因安全担忧放弃发布最新 AI 模型</span></a><span class="dom">nytimes.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenAI cites safety concerns as the reason for not releasing its newest model Astra, highlighting the ongoing risk-management debate around powerful AI capabilities.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 称出于安全担忧不发布最新的 Astra 模型，突显了在快速释放能力与负责任风险管理之间的权衡。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenAI cites safety concerns as the reason for not releasing its newest model Astra, highlighting the ongoing risk-management debate around powerful AI capabilities. The decision could temper market expectations and influence industry norms around AI deployment.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 称出于安全担忧不发布最新的 Astra 模型，突显了在快速释放能力与负责任风险管理之间的权衡。此决定或影响市场预期并对业界的 AI 部署规范产生影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.lesswrong.com/posts/Nm4ewbYovtjq69dvH/pacing-the-frontier-is-not-the-actual-goal-for-ai-labs" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Pacing the Frontier is not the actual goal for AI labs</span><span class='zh'>‘把控前沿节奏’并非 AI 实验室的真正目标</span></a><span class="dom">lesswrong.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The essay argues that AI labs should not frame progress as simply &#x27;pacing the frontier&#x27;; instead, prioritize safety, reliability, governance, and real-world value to avoid reckless speed and misaligned systems.</span><span class='zh'>这篇文章认为 AI 实验室不应把“把控前沿节奏”作为唯一目标，而应优先考虑安全性、可靠性与治理，以及对现实世界的价值，以避免过快推进带来的风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The essay argues that AI labs should not frame progress as simply &#x27;pacing the frontier&#x27;; instead, prioritize safety, reliability, governance, and real-world value to avoid reckless speed and misaligned systems.</span><span class='zh'>这篇文章认为 AI 实验室不应把“把控前沿节奏”作为唯一目标，而应优先考虑安全性、可靠性与治理，以及对现实世界的价值，以避免过快推进带来的风险。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://arstechnica.com/science/2026/09/scientists-solve-1840s-space-weather-mystery/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Scientists solve 1840s space weather mystery</span><span class='zh'>科学家破解1840年代太空天气之谜</span></a><span class="dom">arstechnica.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers solve a century-old space weather puzzle by reanalyzing 1840s solar activity, refining models of solar storms and their impact on Earth.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员通过重新分析1840年代的太阳活动，破解了一个百年未解的太空天气难题，改进了对太阳风暴及其对地球影响的模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers solve a century-old space weather puzzle by reanalyzing 1840s solar activity, refining models of solar storms and their impact on Earth. The finding improves understanding of historical aurora data and informs modern preparedness.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员通过重新分析1840年代的太阳活动，破解了一个百年未解的太空天气难题，改进了对太阳风暴及其对地球影响的模型。这一发现有助于重建历史极光记录并提升对现代空间天气的应对。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.getprivisy.com/blog/sb-923-ccpa-right-to-delete-signed" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>SB 923 is Law: CCPA deletion rights now reach third-party data</span><span class='zh'>SB 923 成法：CCPA 删除权扩展至第三方数据</span></a><span class="dom">getprivisy.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>SB 923 broadens CCPA deletion rights to data held by third parties, expanding consumer control and imposing new compliance burdens on processors and data brokers.</span><span class='zh'>SB 923 将删除权扩展至第三方数据，扩大了消费者对自身数据的控制，并给数据处理者与数据经纪商带来新的合规负担。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>SB 923 broadens CCPA deletion rights to data held by third parties, expanding consumer control and imposing new compliance burdens on processors and data brokers. Expect policy debates and new tooling for data deletion workflows.</span><span class='zh'>SB 923 将删除权扩展至第三方数据，扩大了消费者对自身数据的控制，并给数据处理者与数据经纪商带来新的合规负担。未来或引发合规工具与流程的升级。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New archival work suggests Elizabeth I’s sharp letters were edited or revised by male secretaries to amplify temper and authority. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://iain.rocks/blog/dont-couple-your-go-code-to-github" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Don&#x27;t couple your Go code to GitHub</span><span class='zh'>不要把 Go 代码与 GitHub 捆绑在一起</span></a><span class="dom">iain.rocks</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author argues against tightly coupling Go projects to GitHub, advocating for alternative hosting, CI strategies, and dependency management to improve resilience and portability.</span><span class='zh'>作者主张不要让 Go 项目过度绑定到 GitHub，建议采用替代托管、CI 策略与依赖管理来提升韧性与可移植性，并考虑灾难恢复与供应商独立性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author argues against tightly coupling Go projects to GitHub, advocating for alternative hosting, CI strategies, and dependency management to improve resilience and portability. It urges thinking about disaster recovery and vendor independence.</span><span class='zh'>作者主张不要让 Go 项目过度绑定到 GitHub，建议采用替代托管、CI 策略与依赖管理来提升韧性与可移植性，并考虑灾难恢复与供应商独立性。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Nissan&#x27;s third-generation e-POWER refines its range-extended electrified setup, boosting efficiency and drive smoothness while staying non-plug-in.</span><span class='zh'>日产第三代 e-POWER 对其增程式电动系统进行了改进，提升了效率和平顺性，同时保持非插电特性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.inkandswitch.com/essay/malleable-software/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Malleable software: Restoring user agency in a world of locked-down apps (2025)</span><span class='zh'>可塑性软件：在被封锁的应用世界中重塑用户自主权（2025）</span></a><span class="dom">inkandswitch.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Contends for malleable software that users can inspect, modify, and repair, even as apps lock down features.</span><span class='zh'>主张让软件具备可塑性，用户可以查看、修改和修复，即使应用方锁定功能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Contends for malleable software that users can inspect, modify, and repair, even as apps lock down features. Proposes design norms and platform incentives to reclaim user agency, privacy, and resilience against vendor-driven lock-in.</span><span class='zh'>主张让软件具备可塑性，用户可以查看、修改和修复，即使应用方锁定功能。提出设计原则和平台激励，以重获用户自主权、隐私和对抗供应商锁定的韧性。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The post warns that AT TIME ZONE &#x27;UTC&#x27; in Postgres does not behave as many expect, leading to subtle timestamp errors. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Imp is a full port of DSPy to the BEAM VM, bringing DSPy’s data-science capabilities to Erlang and Elixir ecosystems.</span><span class='zh'>Imp 将 DSPy 完整移植到 BEAM 虚拟机，将 DSPy 的数据科学能力带入 Erlang/Elixir 生态。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Imp is a full port of DSPy to the BEAM VM, bringing DSPy’s data-science capabilities to Erlang and Elixir ecosystems. The project enables BEAM friendly probabilistic programming workflows and leverages the actor model for parallelism across cores.</span><span class='zh'>Imp 将 DSPy 完整移植到 BEAM 虚拟机，将 DSPy 的数据科学能力带入 Erlang/Elixir 生态。该项目为 BEAM 上的概率编程和并行工作流提供更友好的实现，利用 actor 模型在多核上实现并行。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://shnatsel.github.io/state-of-simd-rust-2026/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The state of SIMD in Rust in 2026</span><span class='zh'>Rust 在 2026 年的 SIMD 状态</span></a><span class="dom">shnatsel.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Rust&#x27;s SIMD story in 2026 shows the portable SIMD API maturing, with broader compiler support and ecosystem tools.</span><span class='zh'>到 2026 年，Rust 的 SIMD 生态逐步成熟，便携式 SIMD API 得到更广泛的编译器支持和工具链。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Rust&#x27;s SIMD story in 2026 shows the portable SIMD API maturing, with broader compiler support and ecosystem tools. Developers gain clearer performance paths for vectorized code across CPUs, but stabilizing features and crate parity remains a challenge.</span><span class='zh'>到 2026 年，Rust 的 SIMD 生态逐步成熟，便携式 SIMD API 得到更广泛的编译器支持和工具链。面向向量化的性能路径变得更清晰，但稳定特性与生态配套仍有挑战。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.blog/engineering/architecture-optimization/improving-site-performance-by-shipping-more-css/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Improving site performance by shipping more CSS</span><span class='zh'>通过投放更多 CSS 提升站点性能</span></a><span class="dom">github.blog</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues that increasing the amount of CSS delivered upfront can improve page speed by reducing render-blocking and extra network trips, especially when styles are reused across pages.</span><span class='zh'>文章认为在初始加载中投放更多 CSS 可以通过减少渲染阻塞和重复请求来提升页面速度，尤其是跨页面大量复用样式时。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues that increasing the amount of CSS delivered upfront can improve page speed by reducing render-blocking and extra network trips, especially when styles are reused across pages. It offers data-backed guidance on caching strategies, specificity, and bundle sizing to balance initial paint with repeat visits.</span><span class='zh'>文章认为在初始加载中投放更多 CSS 可以通过减少渲染阻塞和重复请求来提升页面速度，尤其是跨页面大量复用样式时。给出关于缓存策略、选择性和打包规模的权衡要点，帮助兼顾首屏和后续访问。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>86</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49856193' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>69</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-49861755" data-id="49861755" data-tags="AI|Business" data-score="63" data-comments="17" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49861755" data-time="1790466541">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://this.os.isfine.org/blog/posts/what-reverse-engineering-and-modernising-an-old-war-game-tells-us-about-the-econ/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>What reversing, modernising old games tells us about the economic impact of AI</span><span class='zh'>逆向工程与现代化老游戏揭示 AI 的经济影响</span></a><span class="dom">this.os.isfine.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece uses reverse engineering and modernization of an old war game to draw lessons about AI&#x27;s economic impact, including productivity and labor displacement patterns.</span><span class='zh'>通过对一款旧战争游戏的逆向工程与现代化，该文讨论 AI 对经济的影响，例如生产力提升与劳动替代的模式，并分析维护成本与新开发成本如何推动 AI 的采用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece uses reverse engineering and modernization of an old war game to draw lessons about AI&#x27;s economic impact, including productivity and labor displacement patterns. It argues how maintenance vs new development costs shape AI adoption.</span><span class='zh'>通过对一款旧战争游戏的逆向工程与现代化，该文讨论 AI 对经济的影响，例如生产力提升与劳动替代的模式，并分析维护成本与新开发成本如何推动 AI 的采用。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues that C&#x27;s varied integer widths were deliberate to balance portability, performance, and hardware realities. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://chillphysicsenjoyer.substack.com/p/a-new-experiment-meta-strategy" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A New Experiment Meta-Strategy</span><span class='zh'>一种新的实验元策略</span></a><span class="dom">chillphysicsenjoyer.substack.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Proposes a meta-strategy for experiments that maximizes information gain under limits like time and budget.</span><span class='zh'>提出一套以信息增益最大化为目标的实验元策略，帮助研究者在时间、经费等约束下更高效地进行实验。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Proposes a meta-strategy for experiments that maximizes information gain under limits like time and budget. The approach aims to improve reproducibility and decision-making in research by structuring iterative testing.</span><span class='zh'>提出一套以信息增益最大化为目标的实验元策略，帮助研究者在时间、经费等约束下更高效地进行实验。该方法旨在提升可复现性和决策质量。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://icm.museum/blog/?p=207" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>KICKI: a DECsystem1060</span><span class='zh'>Kicki：一台 DECsystem1060 — Interim Computer Museum</span></a><span class="dom">icm.museum</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Kicki is a DECsystem-1060 featured in an Interim Computer Museum, offering a window into early mainframe-era computing.</span><span class='zh'>Kicki 是在 Interim Computer Museum 展出的 DECsystem-1060，提供了了解早期主机时代计算机的窗口。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Kicki is a DECsystem-1060 featured in an Interim Computer Museum, offering a window into early mainframe-era computing. The post likely covers hardware quirks, historical significance, and how these collections help preservation and education.</span><span class='zh'>Kicki 是在 Interim Computer Museum 展出的 DECsystem-1060，提供了了解早期主机时代计算机的窗口。文章介绍了其硬件特性、历史意义，以及这些收藏如何帮助保存与教育。</span></p>
